# Pre-change measurements — implement-start baseline (two-slot budget, slot 1 of 2)

- Authority: `tasks.md` T003 (FR-011 / FR-020 / SC-001 / SC-004 / SC-005 inputs);
  `contracts/tier-selection.contract.md` C-015.
- Recorded: 2026-11-11, worktree
  `/storage/project/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/.specify/.tmp/sdd-loop/worktrees/098-suite-speed-optimizations`,
  tree head `ec1620289` (WP-8 close; this WP-9 recording in progress).
- Implement-start tree: `b3da3a26d` (the worktree base). The feature branch's earlier commits
  added `.specify/specs/098-suite-speed-optimizations/` only; `tests/` was identical to the
  base commit when implement started.

## Timing disclosure (why slot 1 is recorded at WP-9)

The package map (`tasks.md` § Work Package Mapping) assigns T003 to WP-9, the last package, so the
slot-1 run below executes on the post-WP-8 tree rather than literally before WP-1. The pre-change
runner ran the full selection by construction; the current runner reproduces that same full
selection with `--full` (tier-selection C-001 / C-006: the full tier keeps 086's expressions
unchanged). The selection-integrity reading is therefore re-derived against a real base-commit
tree extraction — section 2 — so a lost or renamed test cannot hide behind the timing.

**Superseded for SC-005 by review-repair r1 (2026-11-11, A3/I3)**: a true pre-change baseline was
rebuilt on a throwaway worktree at the implement-entry commit (`af73f65f1`, parent of the first
implement commit `0d1f7a1e4`) and re-measured against the final tree — see **§ 7 below**. The
section-1 slots below remain the implement-stage readings and the `--names-out` source for
`baseline-failed.txt`; the SC-005 wall comparison uses § 7's pair.

## 1. Slot 1 of 2 — full-suite budget run

- Command: `bash scripts/bash/run-tests.sh --full --names-out .specify/.tmp/wp9/baseline-failed.txt`
  (run-time output landed under `.specify/.tmp/wp9/` and was copied here afterwards — corpus-write
  hazard: nothing was written under `.specify/specs` while the run was in flight).
- Exit `1`; wall `70.99 s`; pytest summary `287 failed, 8299 passed, 2 skipped in 47.55 s`.
  Complete stdout + stderr archived at `test-evidence/run-logs/slot1-full.log.gz` (header:
  command, exit, wall).
- Mode lines: `# test runner: mode=parallel workers=auto dist=load passes=2 tier=full excluded_slow=0`
  and `# test runner exceptions: 1 declared serial test(s)`.
- `--names-out` reading: 287 FAILED names, copied verbatim to
  `.specify/specs/098-suite-speed-optimizations/baseline-failed.txt`
  (sha256 `1887c9cf7556ad419b0d652eeb3c5443497068031df4beb7b15af4159199da44`). The two pre-existing names from S-0.5 are present:
  `test_c5_gate_budget_is_unchanged_by_the_owner_document` and
  `test_c10_openapi_clauses_are_method_keys_and_the_total_matches_the_census`.
- The five contract files this feature adds are green in this run; `test_obligation_11` is green
  (the baseline block below is in place before the run, so the guard reads it).
- Reading: the 287 names are the pre-existing failing set of this branch's full suite, compared by
  name at closure (`comm -13`), never by count.


## 2. Implement-start full-tier collection id set (C-015 input)

- Tree: `git archive b3da3a26d | tar -x -C .specify/.tmp/wp9/before-tree` (old runner: no tier,
  full selection by construction).
- Command: `cd .specify/.tmp/wp9/before-tree && bash scripts/bash/run-tests.sh --serial --collect-only -q`
- Exit `0`; wall `12.92 s`; pytest summary `8543 tests collected in 11.55 s`
  (re-derives the generation-time P-1 reading `8543`).
- Two parametrized ids (`tests/contract/test_teaching_surfaces.py::test_c4_skill_retired_phrases_absent[...]`)
  embed the scratch tree's absolute root; they are normalized to the worktree root so the id set is
  tree-location independent. One id per line, no duplicates.
- Current full-tier reading for T022: `bash scripts/bash/run-tests.sh --serial --full --collect-only -q`
  → exit `0`, wall `2.99 s`, `8589 tests collected in 1.79 s`. The name-level diff is recorded in
  `test-evidence/full-collection-diff.txt`: removed `0`, added `46` (the five new contract files of
  this feature).

## 3. S-0.4 — `test_client_metrics.py` pre-change wall

- Tree/command: base tree (`b3da3a26d` extraction), same host and day, two workers:
  `bash scripts/bash/run-tests.sh --workers 2 tests/contract/test_client_metrics.py -q`
- Exit `0`; wall `8.11 s`; pytest summary `16 passed in 6.76 s`; old mode line carries no tier fields.
- Reading: the wall contains the eight `time.sleep(1.1)` ordering waits (8.8 s of fixed waits spread
  over two workers). The converted reading is recorded in `test-evidence/event-wait-green.txt` (C-010).

## 4. S-0.5 — corpus-pair pre-change pass/fail names

- Tree/command: base tree (`b3da3a26d` extraction):
  `bash scripts/bash/run-tests.sh --workers 2 --names-out <scratch>/corpus-before-failed.txt tests/contract/test_coverage_receipt.py tests/contract/test_clause_coverage.py -q`
- Exit `1`; wall `40.83 s`; pytest summary `2 failed, 62 passed in 39.45 s`.
- FAILED names (2, the pre-existing baseline pair):
  - `tests/contract/test_clause_coverage.py::test_c5_gate_budget_is_unchanged_by_the_owner_document`
  - `tests/contract/test_clause_coverage.py::test_c10_openapi_clauses_are_method_keys_and_the_total_matches_the_census`
- `scan-confirmation-gates.py --summary` on the base tree reports `blocking confirmation gates: 28`
  against the test's stale pin `27`, so c5's red is pre-existing at the base commit, not a 098 effect.

## 5. P-3 — `time.sleep` census (pre-change)

- Command: `git grep -n -e 'time\.sleep' b3da3a26d -- 'tests/**/*.py' 'tests/*.py'`
- Exit `0`; wall `0.02 s`; **20 occurrences in 10 files** (matches generation-time P-3).
- Per file: `test_client_metrics.py` 8, `test_feedback_probe_cli.py` 1,
  `test_line_envelope_contract.py` 2, `test_store_state_protocol.py` 2,
  `fixtures/line_fixtures.py` 1, `fixtures/sdd_loop_efficiency/driver-stall-fixture.py` 1,
  `test_sdd_loop_efficiency_runner.py` 1, `test_session_kill_reattach.py` 2,
  `unit/test_concurrency_check_engine.py` 1, `unit/test_line_registry_engine.py` 1.
- Post-change context (same command at `HEAD`): `8` occurrences in `6` files; the six-file scope
  carries zero synchronization sleeps (SC-008, `test-evidence/event-wait-green.txt`).

## 6. P-4 — `subprocess.run` census (pre-change)

- Command: `git grep -n -e 'subprocess\.run' b3da3a26d -- 'tests/**/*.py' 'tests/*.py'`
- Exit `0`; wall `0.02 s`; **499 source lines in 229 files** (matches generation-time P-4; source
  lines are not call sites — the AST census behind `test-evidence/inprocess-conversion.md` owns the
  final denominator).
- Post-change context (same command at `HEAD`): `359` source lines in `160` files.

## 7. Review-repair r1 — rebuilt pre-change baseline → final tree (SC-005's referent)

- Date: 2026-11-11; same host as the slot-1 readings; same runner form on both legs.
- **Why**: A3/I3 — the slot-1 run in § 1 executed at WP-9 on the post-WP-8 tree, so the
  original "≤ 110 % of the baseline" reading compared post-change against post-change. This
  section re-constitutes slot 1 on its proper pre-change tree and re-measures the final tree.
- **Rebuild tree**: throwaway worktree `git worktree add --detach
  /storage/project/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/.specify/.tmp/098-prechange-af73f65f1 af73f65f1`
  (implement-entry commit = parent of the first implement commit `0d1f7a1e4`).
  `git diff --stat b3da3a26d af73f65f1 -- tests/ scripts/ pyproject.toml` = the constitution
  version pin in `tests/contract/test_fast_fail_discipline.py` only — the tests, runner and
  selection surface equal the base commit. The worktree was removed after its run.
- **Pre-change leg**: `bash scripts/bash/run-tests.sh --names-out
  <scratch>/prechange-baseline-failed.txt` (the old runner selects everything by
  construction — no `--full` flag exists at that tree) → exit `1`, wall **76.52 s**,
